g^{(i)}\right)\\
&\qquad+\frac{\bar{\Lambda}}{a_{i}^{2}}\left(2\mathchoice{\delta g^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}-\frac{1}{2}\delta^{\mu}_{\nu}\delta g^{(i)}\right)\;.\end{split}

(51)

Furthermore, from the background equations (
50
) we have
\mathchoice{\bar{W}^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\bar{W}^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\bar{W}^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\bar{W}^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}=\delta^{\mu}_{\nu}M_{i}^{D-2}\bar{\Lambda}/a_{i}^{2}
, and hence:

\kappa_{i}^{2}\mathchoice{\bar{W}^{{{(i)\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.82239pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.82239pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.35754pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9697pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\bar{W}^{{{(i)\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.82239pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.82239pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.35754pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9697pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\bar{W}^{{{(i)\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.82239pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.82239pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.35754pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9697pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\bar{W}^{{{(i)\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.82239pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.82239pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.35754pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9697pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}\left[\mathchoice{\delta g^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}-\frac{1}{2}\delta^{\mu}_{\lambda}\delta g^{(i)}\right]=\frac{\bar{\Lambda}}{a_{i}^{2}}\left[\mathchoice{\delta g^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}-\frac{1}{2}\delta^{\mu}_{\nu}\delta g^{(i)}\right]

(52)

Regarding the mass terms, the fact that
S_{i\rightarrow j}
is proportional to the identity matrix hugely simplifies things, as all contractions with the generalised deltas in the
\mathcal{B}
-tensors may be evaluated using Eq. (
9
) to yield:

\displaystyle[\mathcal{B}_{i,j}^{(+)}]^{\mu\;\;\alpha}_{\;\;\nu\;\;\beta}

\displaystyle=\sigma_{i,j}^{(+)}\delta^{\mu\alpha}_{\nu\beta}\;,

(53)

\displaystyle[\mathcal{B}_{i,k}^{(-)}]^{\mu\;\;\alpha}_{\;\;\nu\;\;\beta}

\displaystyle=\sigma_{i,k}^{(-)}\delta^{\mu\alpha}_{\nu\beta}\;,

(54)

where we have defined the following combinations of interaction coefficients and conformal factors:

\displaystyle\sigma^{(+)}_{i,j}

\displaystyle=\sum_{m=0}^{D}\beta_{m}^{(i,j)}\binom{D-2}{m-1}\left(\frac{a_{j}}{a_{i}}\right)^{m-1}\;,

(55)

\displaystyle\sigma^{(-)}_{i,k}

\displaystyle=\sum_{m=0}^{D}\beta_{D-m}^{(k,i)}\binom{D-2}{m-1}\left(\frac{a_{k}}{a_{i}}\right)^{m-1}\;.

(56)

These two parameters appear ubiquitously in multi-gravity – we will see them appearing in cosmological and black hole perturbations later on as well. The plus and minus variants associated to any given pair of metrics are related to one another by:

\sigma^{(-)}_{j,i}=\left(\frac{a_{i}}{a_{j}}\right)^{D-2}\sigma^{(+)}_{i,j}\;,

(57)

informing us that the
\sigma_{i,j}^{(\pm)}
parameters live on the interaction links coupling
g^{(i)}_{\mu\nu}
and
g^{(j)}_{\mu\nu}
.

Substituting Eqs. (
51
), (
52
), (
53
) and (
54
) into Eqs. (
45
), one finds that the linearised field equations around a proportional background are:

\begin{split}&\frac{\mathchoice{\bar{\mathcal{E}}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{\bar{\mathcal{E}}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{\bar{\mathcal{E}}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[ g^{(i)\beta}_{\;\;\;\;\;\alpha}}{a_{i}^{2}}-\frac{2\bar{\Lambda}}{a_{i}^{2}(D-2)}\left(\mathchoice{\delta g^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}-\frac{1}{2}\delta^{\mu}_{\nu}\delta g^{(i)}\right)\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\Bigg\{\sum_{j}\kappa_{i}\frac{a_{j}}{a_{i}}\sigma_{i,j}^{(+)}\bigg[\kappa_{i}\left(\mathchoice{\delta g^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}-\delta^{\mu}_{\nu}\delta g^{(i)}\right)\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\kappa_{j}\left(\mathchoice{\delta g^{{{(j)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[14.82591pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.82591pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.06192pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.47281pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\delta g^{{{(j)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[14.82591pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.82591pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.06192pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.47281pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\delta g^{{{(j)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[14.82591pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.82591pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.06192pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.47281pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\delta g^{{{(j)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[14.82591pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.82591pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.06192pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.47281pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}-\delta^{\mu}_{\nu}\delta g^{(j)}\right)\bigg]\\
&\qquad+\sum_{k}\kappa_{i}\frac{a_{k}}{a_{i}}\sigma_{i,k}^{(-)}\bigg[\kappa_{i}\left(\mathchoice{\delta g^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}-\delta^{\mu}_{\nu}\delta g^{(i)}\right)\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\kappa_{k}\left(\mathchoice{\delta g^{{{(k)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[15.53252pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.53252pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.46878pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.76343pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\delta g^{{{(k)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[15.53252pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.53252pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.46878pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.76343pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\delta g^{{{(k)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[15.53252pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.53252pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.46878pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.76343pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\delta g^{{{(k)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[15.53252pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.53252pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.46878pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.76343pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}-\delta^{\mu}_{\nu}\delta g^{(k)}\right)\bigg]\Bigg\}=0\;.\end{split}

(58)

We can rewrite this so that all tensorial quantities behave as if they lived in the
common
background of
\bar{g}_{\mu\nu}
, by defining:

\mathchoice{\delta\tilde{g}^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\delta\tilde{g}^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\delta\tilde{g}^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\delta\tilde{g}^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}\equiv a_{i}^{2}\mathchoice{\delta g^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}=\bar{g}^{\mu\lambda}\delta g^{(i)}_{\lambda\nu}\;,

(59)

which has its indices manipulated with
\bar{g}_{\mu\nu}
, rather than
g^{(i)}_{\mu\nu}
. Rewriting the above set of field equations in terms of the tilded metric perturbations, then lowering an index using
\bar{g}_{\mu\nu}
, one arrives at:

\begin{split}&\mathchoice{\bar{\mathcal{E}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{\bar{\mathcal{E}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{\bar{\mathcal{E}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{\bar{\mathcal{E}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}\delta\tilde{g}^{(i)\beta}_{\;\;\;\;\;\alpha}-\frac{2\bar{\Lambda}}{D-2}\left(\delta\tilde{g}^{(i)}_{\mu\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\bar{g}_{\mu\nu}\delta\tilde{g}^{(i)}\right)\\
&+\frac{a_{i}^{2}}{2}\Bigg\{\sum_{j}\kappa_{i}\sigma_{i,j}^{(+)}\bigg[\kappa_{i}\frac{a_{j}}{a_{i}}\left(\delta\tilde{g}^{(i)}_{\mu\nu}-\bar{g}_{\mu\nu}\delta\tilde{g}^{(i)}\right)\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\kappa_{j}\frac{a_{i}}{a_{j}}\left(\delta\tilde{g}^{(j)}_{\mu\nu}-\bar{g}_{\mu\nu}\delta\tilde{g}^{(j)}\right)\bigg]\\
&\qquad+\sum_{k}\kappa_{i}\sigma_{i,k}^{(-)}\bigg[\kappa_{i}\frac{a_{k}}{a_{i}}\left(\delta\tilde{g}^{(i)}_{\mu\nu}-\bar{g}_{\mu\nu}\delta\tilde{g}^{(i)}\right)\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\kappa_{k}\frac{a_{i}}{a_{k}}\left(\delta\tilde{g}^{(k)}_{\mu\nu}-\bar{g}_{\mu\nu}\delta\tilde{g}^{(k)}\right)\bigg]\Bigg\}=0\;.\end{split}

(60)

One can check that these equations agree with the linearised multi-metric field equations given in
prop_bg_multigrav
, for the two classes of theory graph they consider (‘star-type’ and ‘chain-type’ interactions); our Eqs. (
60
) constitute the appropriate generalisation to arbitrary interaction structures with any number of positively and negatively oriented interactions per metric.

A final step we can take, given that we have already rewritten everything with respect to the common background metric
\bar{g}_{\mu\nu}
, is to ensure that the perturbations are all canonically normalised with respect to
\bar{g}_{\mu\nu}
. This requires that we take:

\delta\tilde{g}^{(i)}_{\mu\nu}=a_{i}h^{(i)}_{\mu\nu}\;,

(61)

With this choice, the linearised field equations (
60
) become:

\begin{split}\mathchoice{\bar{\mathcal{E}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{\bar{\mathcal{E}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{\bar{\mathcal{E}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{\bar{\mathcal{E}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}h^{(i)\beta}_{\;\;\;\;\;\alpha}&-\frac{2\bar{\Lambda}}{D-2}\left(h^{(i)}_{\mu\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\bar{g}_{\mu\nu}h^{(i)}\right)\\
&+\frac{\mathcal{M}_{ij}^{2}}{2}\left(h^{(j)}_{\mu\nu}-\bar{g}_{\mu\nu}h^{(j)}\right)=0\;,\end{split}

(62)

which is precisely in the multi-FP form alluded to by Eq. (
2
), where the mass matrix has components:

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{ii}^{2}

\displaystyle=\kappa_{i}^{2}a_{i}^{2}\left(\sum_{j}\frac{a_{j}}{a_{i}}\sigma_{i,j}^{(+)}+\sum_{k}\frac{a_{k}}{a_{i}}\sigma_{i,k}^{(-)}\right)\;,

(63)

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{ji}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{a_{j}}{a_{i}}\right)^{4-D}\mathcal{M}_{ij}^{2}=-\kappa_{i}\kappa_{j}a_{i}^{2}\sigma_{i,j}^{(+)}\;,

(64)

and all tensorial quantities behave as if they lived in the common background of
\bar{g}_{\mu\nu}
. It is a simple check to show that there always exists a single massless eigenvector (i.e. with eigenvalue
m_{0}^{2}=0
) scaling as
O^{j0}\propto a_{j}/\kappa_{j}
, irrespective of any choices for
\beta_{m}^{(i,j)}
, corresponding to the surviving diagonal copy of diffeomorphism invariance in the theory. This is in accordance with the no-go theorem mentioned in the introduction forbidding theories of multiple interacting massless spin-2 fields
no_interacting_massless_gravitons
.

IV.2
Cosmological backgrounds

Now let us turn to a background in which the perturbation structure is somewhat more involved, namely, that of an expanding FLRW universe, where the most general homogeneous and isotropic ansatz one can make for the metrics at background level (in
D=4
dimensions) is:

g^{(i)}_{\mu\nu}\differential x^{\mu}\differential x^{\nu}=-c_{i}^{2}(t)\differential t^{2}+a_{i}^{2}(t)\left[\frac{\differential r^{2}}{1-kr^{2}}+r^{2}\differential\Omega_{2}^{2}\right]\;,

(65)

where
c_{i}(t)
are the
lapses
,
a_{i}(t)
are the
scale factors
,
\differential\Omega_{2}^{2}
is the line element for a 2-sphere, and it can be shown that the Bianchi constraints (
17
) force the spatial curvature
k
to be the same for all metrics
k=0
. One of the lapses, say, on the distinguished metric
g^{(i_{*})}_{\mu\nu}
, can be fixed to
c_{i_{*}}(t)=1
throughout all time by choosing a preferred time coordinate; ordinarily one would choose
g^{(i_{*})}_{\mu\nu}
to be the metric to which matter couples, so that
t
then corresponds to the cosmic time.

The background cosmology of multi-gravity, with the ansatz (
65
) for the metrics, is discussed in detail in
KWThesis
. Essentially, solutions split into three branches depending on how the Bianchi constraints are satisfied, of which two are unstable to nonlinear ghosts and growing tensor modes
nonlinear_instability_FRW
;
nonlinear_cosmo_stability
;
general_mass
, but the third (which requires
c_{i}=\dot{a}_{i}/\dot{a}_{i_{*}}
) is stable and has particularly interesting (and potentially observationally relevant
analytical_constraints_bigravity
;
observational_constraints_bigravity
;
combining_cosmo_local_bounds_bigravity
;
HubbleTensionBigravity
;
Bigravity_DESI
) phenomenology e.g. there is a dynamical effective dark energy component arising due to the
W
-tensors that has a phantom equation of state in the distant past but which later relaxes to an effective cosmological constant once all external matter has diluted away.

For our purposes, all we wish to do is to use our perturbation formalism to compute the effective mass terms around this FLRW background. To this end, immediately from Eq. (
65
) one sees that the building-block matrices
S_{i\rightarrow j}
take the simple form:

S_{i\rightarrow j}=\text{diag}\left(\frac{c_{j}}{c_{i}},\frac{a_{j}}{a_{i}},\frac{a_{j}}{a_{i}},\frac{a_{j}}{a_{i}}\right)\;,

(66)

from which, by substituting into Eqs. (
42
) and (
43
), one finds the following perturbation structure:

g^{{{(i,k)0}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.11525pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.11525pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.01552pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.58252pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i,k)0}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.11525pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.11525pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.01552pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.58252pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i,k)0}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.11525pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.11525pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.01552pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.58252pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}}}}\delta^{m}_{n}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{a_{k}}{a_{i}}\varsigma_{i,k}^{(-)}(t)\left(\delta^{m}_{n}{\delta g}^{(i,k)k}_{\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;k}-\mathchoice{\delta g^{{{(i,k)m}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[24.22523pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[24.22523pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[13.86778pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.90556pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{n}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i,k)m}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[24.22523pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[24.22523pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[13.86778pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.90556pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{n}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i,k)m}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[24.22523pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[24.22523pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[13.86778pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.90556pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{n}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i,k)m}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[24.22523pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[24.22523pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[13.86778pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.90556pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{n}}}}\right)\;,

(72)

\displaystyle[\mathcal{B}_{i,k}^{(-)}]^{0\;\;\alpha}_{\;\;m\;\;\beta}(\bar{S}_{i\rightarrow k})^{\beta}_{\;\lambda}\mathchoice{\delta g^{{{(i,k)\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.8722pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.8722pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.42386pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.87419pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i,k)\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.8722pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.8722pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.42386pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.87419pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i,k)\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.8722pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.8722pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.42386pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.87419pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i,k)\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.8722pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.8722pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.42386pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.87419pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}=-\frac{c_{k}}{c_{i}}\sigma_{i,k}^{(-)}(t)\mathchoice{\delta g^{{{(i,k)0}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.11525pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.11525pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.01552pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.58252pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i,k)0}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.11525pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.11525pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.01552pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.58252pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i,k)0}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.11525pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.11525pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.01552pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.58252pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i,k)0}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.11525pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.11525pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.01552pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.58252pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}}\;,

(73)

\displaystyle[\mathcal{B}_{i,k}^{(-)}]^{m\;\;\alpha}_{\;\;0\;\;\beta}(\bar{S}_{i\rightarrow k})^{\beta}_{\;\lambda}\mathchoice{\delta g^{{{(i,k)\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.8722pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.8722pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.42386pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.87419pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i,k)\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.8722pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.8722pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.42386pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.87419pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i,k)\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.8722pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.8722pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.42386pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.87419pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i,k)\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.8722pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.8722pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.42386pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.87419pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}=-\frac{a_{k}}{a_{i}}\sigma_{i,k}^{(-)}(t)\mathchoice{\delta g^{{{(i,k)m}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[24.22523pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[24.22523pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[13.86778pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.90556pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i,k)m}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[24.22523pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[24.22523pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[13.86778pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.90556pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i,k)m}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[24.22523pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[24.22523pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[13.86778pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.90556pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}}}}{\delta g^{{{(i,k)m}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[24.22523pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[24.22523pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[13.86778pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.90556pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}}}}\;,

(74)

for the negatively oriented interactions, where the notation
\delta g^{(i,j)}
is used to refer to either
\delta g^{(i)}
or
\delta g^{(j)}
, and we have defined the following time-dependent parameters:

\displaystyle\sigma_{i,j}^{(+)}(t)

\displaystyle=\beta_{1}^{(i,j)}+2\beta_{2}^{(i,j)}\frac{a_{j}}{a_{i}}+\beta_{3}^{(i,j)}\left(\frac{a_{j}}{a_{i}}\right)^{2}\;,

(75)

\displaystyle\sigma_{i,k}^{(-)}(t)

\displaystyle=\beta_{3}^{(k,i)}+2\beta_{2}^{(k,i)}\frac{a_{k}}{a_{i}}+\beta_{1}^{(k,i)}\left(\frac{a_{k}}{a_{i}}\right)^{2}\;,

(76)

\displaystyle\varsigma_{i,j}^{(+)}(t)

\displaystyle=\beta_{1}^{(i,j)}+\beta_{2}^{(i,j)}\left(\frac{c_{j}}{c_{i}}+\frac{a_{j}}{a_{i}}\right)+\beta_{3}^{(i,j)}\frac{c_{j}}{c_{i}}\frac{a_{j}}{a_{i}}\;,

(77)

\displaystyle\varsigma_{i,k}^{(-)}(t)

\displaystyle=\beta_{3}^{(k,i)}+\beta_{2}^{(k,i)}\left(\frac{c_{k}}{c_{i}}+\frac{a_{k}}{a_{i}}\right)+\beta_{1}^{(k,i)}\frac{c_{k}}{c_{i}}\frac{a_{k}}{a_{i}}\;,

(78)

which are related by:

\displaystyle\sigma_{i,j}^{(+)}(t)

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{a_{j}}{a_{i}}\right)^{2}\sigma_{j,i}^{(-)}(t)\;,

(79)

\displaystyle\varsigma_{i,j}^{(+)}(t)

\displaystyle=\frac{c_{j}}{c_{i}}\frac{a_{j}}{a_{i}}\varsigma_{j,i}^{(-)}(t)\;.

(80)

We note that the
\sigma_{i,j}^{(\pm)}(t)
parameters are simply time-dependent analogues of the constant
\sigma_{i,j}^{(\pm)}
parameters we introduced in Eqs. (
55
) and (
56
) to encode the spin-2 mass matrix around a proportional background, where the constant conformal factors have been replaced by the time-dependent scale factors. The
\varsigma_{i,j}^{(\pm)}
parameters have no such analogue in general, but note that if
a_{i}=c_{i}
(which happens at late times after all external matter has diluted away
analytical_constraints_bigravity
) then the two metrics are actually proportional; in this case,
\sigma_{i,j}^{(\pm)}(t)
and
\varsigma_{i,j}^{(\pm)}(t)
coincide, and the perturbation structure of Eqs. (
67
)–(
74
) reduces down to that of Eqs. (
53
) and (
54
) around a proportional solution, as it should do.

One can check that the effective mass terms in Eqs. (
67
)–(
74
) agree with those appearing in the linearised cosmological field equations of e.g.
structure_growth
, upon splitting up the components of
\delta g^{(i)\mu}_{\;\;\;\;\;\nu}
into their scalar, vector and tensor parts (the remaining terms in the linearised field equations are all GR-like terms coming from the kinetic sector, whose computation is well-understood – see any classic cosmology textbook for details e.g.
WeinbergCosmology
– so we omit them here). However, the increased generality of our approach provides a number of benefits, for example: a nice application of Eqs. (
67
)–(
74
) is that one can derive the Higuchi bound
OG_Higuchi
;
OG_Higuchi_2
;
General_Higuchi_bound
;
Higuchi_bound_massive_grav
;
higuchi_and_gradient
on cosmological spacetimes in a very neat and clean manner, as well as see very clearly why certain branches of cosmological solutions in multi-gravity are unstable – see
KWThesis
for details.

IV.3
Black hole backgrounds

The final example we would like to give concerns the structure of the effective mass terms around black hole backgrounds. Such calculations are important when one wishes to compute e.g. the quasinormal spectrum of a black hole in massive gravity, with a view to comparison against gravitational wave measurements of black hole binaries. To this end, a complete catalogue of the known black hole solutions in generic multi-gravity theories was constructed in
BHs_multigrav
;
BH2
; all such solutions that may be written down analytically can be cast into Kerr-Schild form:

g^{(i)}_{\mu\nu}=a_{i}^{2}\left[g^{(\Lambda)}_{\mu\nu}+2\phi_{i}l_{\mu}l_{\nu}\right]\;,

(81)

where
a_{i}
are again constant conformal factors,
g^{(\Lambda)}_{\mu\nu}
is the metric of AdS, Minkowski or dS space (
\Lambda<0
,
\Lambda=0
,
\Lambda>0
, respectively) in some coordinate system,
\phi_{i}
are scalar functions containing the Schwarzschild radii for each metric (which are in principle independent) and
l
is a vector tangent to a null-geodesic congruence on
g^{(\Lambda)}_{\mu\nu}
– see
BHs_multigrav
for the explicit expressions. The ansatz (
81
) is able to encompass black holes of arbitrary dimension that can rotate in multiple planes; in
D=4
, or without any rotation, it can also account for electric charge.

As in the cosmological case, there are three distinct branches of solutions (this time depending on whether the metrics are simultaneously diagonalisable) and as before two of these are expected to be pathological: they are analogous to the two pathological branches of cosmological solutions in multiple ways, and we in fact believe the underlying source of the pathology to be the same in both cases – see
KWThesis
;
BH2
for a discussion. However, the pathology has not yet been confirmed explicitly, and it is likely that one will need to go to nonlinear order in perturbation theory to see it.

In any case, with the multi-gravity metrics given in the Kerr-Schild form (
81
), the fact that
l
is null (
l_{\mu}l^{\mu}=0
) makes it surprisingly simple to calculate the form of the
S_{i\rightarrow j}
matrices, as there is an early truncation in the expansion of the matrix square root
Rotating_AdS_bigravity
:

(S_{i\rightarrow j})^{\mu}_{\;\nu}=\frac{a_{j}}{a_{i}}\left[\delta^{\mu}_{\nu}-(\phi_{i}-\phi_{j})l^{\mu}l_{\nu}\right]\;.

(82)

Substituting into Eqs. (
42
) and (
43
) and again utilising the null character of
l
, one finds the following perturbation structure at linear order:

\displaystyle[\mathcal{B}_{i,j}^{(+)}]^{\mu\;\;\alpha}_{\;\;\nu\;\;\beta}=\sigma_{i,j}^{(+)}\delta^{\mu\alpha}_{\nu\beta}

\displaystyle+2\frac{a_{j}}{a_{i}}\left(\phi_{i}-\phi_{j}\right)\left(\beta_{2}^{(i,j)}+\frac{a_{j}}{a_{i}}\beta_{3}^{(i,j)}\right)\left(\delta^{[\mu}_{\nu}l^{\alpha]}l_{\beta}+\delta^{[\alpha}_{\beta}l^{\mu]}l_{\nu}\right)\;,

(83)

\displaystyle[\mathcal{B}_{i,k}^{(-)}]^{\mu\;\;\alpha}_{\;\;\nu\;\;\beta}=\sigma_{i,k}^{(-)}\delta^{\mu\alpha}_{\nu\beta}

\displaystyle+2\frac{a_{k}}{a_{i}}\left(\phi_{i}-\phi_{k}\right)\left(\beta_{2}^{(k,i)}+\frac{a_{k}}{a_{i}}\beta_{1}^{(k,i)}\right)\left(\delta^{[\mu}_{\nu}l^{\alpha]}l_{\beta}+\delta^{[\alpha}_{\beta}l^{\mu]}l_{\nu}\right)\;.

(84)

If all the
\phi_{i}
are equal, then clearly all the metrics in Eq. (
81
) are proportional, and Eqs. (
83
) and (
84
) recover the corresponding Eqs. (
53
) and (
54
) for the
\mathcal{B}
-tensors around a proportional solution, as they should. When this is not the case, the perturbation structure is much more involved: in fact, the linearised field equations around non-proportional black hole solutions in multi-gravity have only previously been computed for a background that is 4-dimensional and non-rotating
GL_unified
;
Stability_nonbidiag
;
BH2
. We will show in appendix
B
how to recover those results from our Eqs. (
83
) and (
84
), but one should note that our expressions are far more general: they hold in arbitrary dimension, and constitute the first time the effective mass term around a non-proportional
rotating
black hole background in multi-gravity has been computed, demonstrating the power of our formalism for determining the perturbations.

V
Cubic potential

Finally, we are going to determine the multi-gravity potential to cubic order around an arbitrary background; previously, this calculation has only been performed around a proportional background (see section
IV
) in
D=4

heavy_spin2_DM
, so here we again chart new territory.

The cubic order variation of the potential, considering all terms from the expansion (
32
) that are third-order in the metric perturbations, has 3 contributions:

\displaystyle I_{V}^{(3)}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{ijkl_{4}\ldots l_{D}}\int\kappa_{i}\kappa_{j}\kappa_{k}\binom{D}{3}T_{ijkl_{4}\ldots l_{D}}\varepsilon_{abcd_{4}\ldots d_{D}}

\displaystyle\times\delta e^{(i)a}_{(1)}\wedge\delta e^{(j)b}_{(1)}\wedge\delta e^{(k)c}_{(1)}\wedge\bar{e}^{(l_{4})d_{4}}\wedge\ldots\wedge\bar{e}^{(l_{D})d_{D}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{ijl_{3}\ldots l_{D}}\int\kappa_{i}^{2}\kappa_{j}\binom{D}{2}T_{ijl_{3}\ldots l_{D}}\varepsilon_{abd_{3}\ldots d_{D}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\delta e^{(i)a}_{(2)}\wedge\delta e^{(j)b}_{(1)}\wedge\bar{e}^{(l_{3})d_{3}}\wedge\ldots\wedge\bar{e}^{(l_{D})d_{D}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{il_{2}\ldots l_{D}}\int\kappa_{i}^{3}\binom{D}{1}T_{il_{2}\ldots l_{D}}\varepsilon_{ad_{2}\ldots d_{D}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\delta e^{(i)a}_{(3)}\wedge\bar{e}^{(l_{2})d_{2}}\wedge\ldots\wedge\bar{e}^{(l_{D})d_{D}}\;,

(85)

which can be expanded out using Eqs. (
33
)–(
35
) to yield:

\displaystyle I_{V}^{(3)}=-\sum_{ijkl}\int\differential[]x\sqrt{-\det\bar{g}_{(i)}}\;\;\times

\displaystyle\Bigg[\frac{\kappa_{i}\kappa_{j}\kappa_{k}}{8}\binom{D}{3}T_{ijkl_{4}\ldots l_{D}}\bar{e}^{(i)\mu\nu\rho\lambda_{4}\ldots\lambda_{D}}_{\;\;\;\;abcd_{4}\ldots d_{D}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\delta g^{(i)\alpha}_{\;\;\;\;\;\mu}\delta g^{(j)\beta}_{\;\;\;\;\;\nu}\delta g^{(k)\gamma}_{\;\;\;\;\;\;\rho}\bar{e}^{(i)a}_{\alpha}\bar{e}^{(j)b}_{\beta}\bar{e}^{(k)c}_{\gamma}\bar{e}^{(l_{4})d_{4}}_{\lambda_{4}}\ldots\bar{e}^{(l_{D})d_{D}}_{\lambda_{D}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\kappa_{i}^{2}\kappa_{j}}{16}\binom{D}{2}T_{ijl_{3}\ldots l_{D}}\bar{e}^{(i)\mu\nu\lambda_{3}\ldots\lambda_{D}}_{\;\;\;\;abd_{3}\ldots d_{D}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\delta g^{(i)\alpha}_{\;\;\;\;\;\sigma}\delta g^{(i)\sigma}_{\;\;\;\;\;\mu}\delta g^{(j)\beta}_{\;\;\;\;\;\nu}\bar{e}^{(i)a}_{\alpha}\bar{e}^{(j)b}_{\beta}\bar{e}^{(l_{3})d_{3}}_{\lambda_{3}}\ldots\bar{e}^{(l_{D})d_{D}}_{\lambda_{D}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\kappa_{i}^{3}}{16}\binom{D}{1}T_{il_{2}\ldots l_{D}}\bar{e}^{(i)\mu\lambda_{2}\ldots\lambda_{D}}_{\;\;\;\;ad_{2}\ldots d_{D}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\delta g^{(i)\alpha}_{\;\;\;\;\;\sigma}\delta g^{(i)\sigma}_{\;\;\;\;\;\gamma}\delta g^{(i)\gamma}_{\;\;\;\;\;\mu}\bar{e}^{(i)a}_{\alpha}\bar{e}^{(l_{2})d_{2}}_{\lambda_{2}}\ldots\bar{e}^{(l_{D})d_{D}}_{\lambda_{D}}\Bigg]\;.

(86)

Restricting to pairwise interactions where the DvN condition (
21
) holds, using the symmetry of the
T_{j_{1}\ldots j_{D}}
coefficients, Eqs. (
27
) and (
28
) to relate these coefficients to the metric
\beta_{m}^{(i,j)}
, and Eqs. (
9
) and (
25
) to evaluate products of generalised deltas, one finds the following cubic potential:

\displaystyle I_{V}^{(3)}=\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\int\differential[]x\sqrt{-\det\bar{g}_{(i)}}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{-\frac{\kappa_{i}^{3}}{16}\delta g^{(i)\alpha}_{\;\;\;\;\;\sigma}\delta g^{(i)\sigma}_{\;\;\;\;\;\rho}\delta g^{(i)\rho}_{\;\;\;\;\;\mu}\bar{W}^{(i)\mu}_{\;\;\;\;\;\;\alpha}+\frac{\kappa_{i}^{3}}{32}\mathchoice{\mathcal{A}^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{\mathcal{A}^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.94164pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.45169pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.03694pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{\mathcal{A}^{{{(i)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{ 5

There are some interesting combinatorics at play here: schematically, at quadratic order, the relation (
44
) is
\delta_{(2)}W=\mathcal{A}+\mathcal{B}S
, while at cubic order the relation (
91
) is
\delta_{(3)}W=\mathcal{Z}+2\mathcal{X}S+\mathcal{Y}SS
; the coefficients of the terms on the right hand sides of these expressions form the second and third rows of Pascal’s triangle! Presumably, at quartic order, there will then exist a similar relation involving 4 new 8-index tensor structures on the right hand side of
\delta_{(4)}W=(...)
appearing with coefficients 1,3,3,1.
:

(91)

Again the structure of the interaction terms (
\mathcal{X}
and
\mathcal{Y}
) in terms of the generalised deltas ensures that
\delta g^{(i)0}_{\;\;\;\;\;0}
will always appear in the action as a Lagrange multiplier, and
\delta g^{(i)j}_{\;\;\;\;\;0}
will always appear as an auxiliary field, leading to the necessary constraints that guarantee the removal of the BD ghost in the standard way.

One can check that around a proportional background Eq. (
87
) reduces to the cubic order potential given in
heavy_spin2_DM
. Precisely, when
g^{(i)}_{\mu\nu}=a_{i}^{2}\bar{g}_{\mu\nu}
so that
(\bar{S}_{i\rightarrow j})^{\mu}_{\;\nu}=(a_{j}/a_{i})\delta^{\mu}_{\nu}
, using Eq. (
9
) to evaluate products of deltas, alongside Eqs. (
53
) and (
54
) for the
\mathcal{B}
-tensors, one finds that the relevant new tensor structures are:

\displaystyle[\mathcal{X}_{i,j}^{(+)}]^{\mu\;\;\nu\;\;\rho}_{\;\;\alpha\;\;\beta\;\;\gamma}

\displaystyle=\eta_{i,j}^{(+)}\delta^{\mu\nu\rho}_{\alpha\beta\gamma}\;,

(92)

\displaystyle[\mathcal{Y}_{i,j}^{(+)}]^{\mu\;\;\nu\;\;\rho}_{\;\;\alpha\;\;\beta\;\;\gamma}

\displaystyle=\frac{a_{i}}{a_{j}}\left[\sigma_{i,j}^{(+)}-\eta_{i,j}^{(+)}\right]\delta^{\mu\nu\rho}_{\alpha\beta\gamma}\;,

(93)

\displaystyle[\mathcal{X}_{i,k}^{(-)}]^{\mu\;\;\nu\;\;\rho}_{\;\;\alpha\;\;\beta\;\;\gamma}

\displaystyle=\eta_{i,k}^{(-)}\delta^{\mu\nu\rho}_{\alpha\beta\gamma}\;,

(94)

\displaystyle[\mathcal{Y}_{i,k}^{(-)}]^{\mu\;\;\nu\;\;\rho}_{\;\;\alpha\;\;\beta\;\;\gamma}

\displaystyle=\frac{a_{i}}{a_{k}}\left[\sigma_{i,k}^{(-)}-\eta_{i,k}^{(-)}\right]\delta^{\mu\nu\rho}_{\alpha\beta\gamma}\;,

(95)

where we have defined:

\displaystyle\eta_{i,j}^{(+)}=\sum_{m=0}^{D}\beta_{m}^{(i,j)}\binom{D-3}{m-1}\left(\frac{a_{j}}{a_{i}}\right)^{m-1}\;,

(96)

\displaystyle\eta_{i,k}^{(-)}=\sum_{m=0}^{D}\beta_{D-m}^{(k,i)}\binom{D-3}{m-1}\left(\frac{a_{k}}{a_{i}}\right)^{m-1}\;,

(97)

related by:

\eta_{i,j}^{(+)}=\sigma_{i,j}^{(+)}-\left(\frac{a_{j}}{a_{i}}\right)^{D-2}\eta_{j,i}^{(-)}\;.

(98)

Using Eqs. (
44
) and (
91
) to eliminate
\mathcal{A}
and
\mathcal{Z}
, and ignoring the terms left multiplying
\bar{W}
(which are just contributions from the background curvature), one finds that the spin-2 interaction potential at cubic order reads:

g^{{{(k)\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[14.81918pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.81918pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.04967pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.46405pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\delta g^{{{(k)\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[14.81918pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.81918pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.04967pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.46405pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\delta \displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\kappa_{i}\kappa_{k}^{2}\left(2\delta g^{(i)}\mathchoice{\delta g^{{{(k)\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[14.67947pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.67947pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.93681pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.38344pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\delta g^{{{(k)\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[14.67947pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.67947pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.93681pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.38344pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}-\delta g^{(k)2}\delta g^{(i)}-3\mathchoice{\delta g^{{{(k)\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[15.53252pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.53252pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.46878pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.76343pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\delta g^{{{(k)\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[14.67947pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.67947pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.93681pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.38344pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\rho}}}}{\delta
